pg_restore: error: could not execute query: ERROR:  permission denied for schema public
Command was: CREATE FUNCTION public.aanwijzing_generate(aanwijzingid character varying) RETURNS character varying
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$

DECLARE 

varAanwijzing VARCHAR;

BEGIN

	IF AANWIJZINGID = '0' THEN

	SELECT COALESCE(CASE WHEN LENGTH(AANWIJZING_ID) = 1 THEN ('0' || AANWIJZING_ID) ELSE TRIM(AANWIJZING_ID) END, '01') INTO varAanwijzing

	FROM (SELECT TRIM(TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(MAX(AANWIJZING_ID), '9999') + 1, '9999')) AANWIJZING_ID FROM AANWIJZING WHERE AANWIJZING_PARENT_ID = '0') A;

	   RETURN(varAanwijzing);    

	ELSE

	SELECT  COALESCE(CASE WHEN LENGTH(AANWIJZING_ID) = 1 THEN ('0' || AANWIJZING_ID) ELSE TRIM(AANWIJZING_ID) END, '01') INTO varAanwijzing FROM 

	 (SELECT TRIM(TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(MAX(SUBSTR(AANWIJZING_ID, LENGTH(AANWIJZINGID) + 1, 2)), '9999') + 1, '9999')) AANWIJZING_ID

	  FROM AANWIJZING

	 WHERE LENGTH (AANWIJZING_ID) = LENGTH(AANWIJZINGID) + 2 AND AANWIJZING_ID LIKE AANWIJZINGID || '%') A;

	   RETURN(AANWIJZINGID || varAanwijzing);    

	END IF;

	RETURN varAanwijzing;

END;

$$;

pg_restore: error: could not execute query: ERROR:  permission denied for schema public
Command was: CREATE FUNCTION public.ambil_akta(rekananid integer) RETURNS character varying
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$

DECLARE 

rec RECORD;

RESULT VARCHAR(5000);

BEGIN

    RESULT:='';

    FOR REC IN (SELECT '<strong>Akta Pendirian</strong><br>Nomor : ' || NOMOR NAMA FROM REKANAN_AKTA WHERE REKANAN_ID = REKANANID AND AKTA_TYPE_ID = 1

		    UNION ALL  

		    SELECT 'Tanggal : ' || TO_CHAR(TANGGAL, 'DD-MM-YYYY') FROM REKANAN_AKTA WHERE REKANAN_ID = REKANANID AND AKTA_TYPE_ID = 1

		    UNION ALL  

		    SELECT 'Notaris : ' || NOTARIS FROM REKANAN_AKTA WHERE REKANAN_ID = REKANANID  AND AKTA_TYPE_ID = 1  

		    UNION ALL  

		    SELECT 'Link : ' || '<a href ="../uploads/landasan_hukum/' || PATH_FILE || '" target="new"> download </a>'  FROM REKANAN_AKTA WHERE REKANAN_ID = REKANANID  AND AKTA_TYPE_ID = 1

		    UNION ALL

		    SELECT '<strong>Akta Perubahan Terakhir</strong><br>Nomor : ' || NOMOR NAMA FROM REKANAN_AKTA WHERE REKANAN_ID = REKANANID AND AKTA_TYPE_ID = 2

		    UNION ALL  

		    SELECT 'Tanggal : ' || TO_CHAR(TANGGAL, 'DD-MM-YYYY') FROM REKANAN_AKTA WHERE REKANAN_ID = REKANANID AND AKTA_TYPE_ID =2

		    UNION ALL  

		    SELECT 'Notaris : ' || NOTARIS FROM REKANAN_AKTA WHERE REKANAN_ID = REKANANID  AND AKTA_TYPE_ID = 2 

		    UNION ALL  

		    SELECT 'Link : ' || '<a href ="../uploads/landasan_hukum/' || PATH_FILE || '" target="new"> download </a>'  FROM REKANAN_AKTA WHERE REKANAN_ID = REKANANID  AND AKTA_TYPE_ID = 2 

        )

    LOOP

        IF RESULT IS NULL THEN

            RESULT := REC.NAMA;

        ELSE        

            RESULT := RESULT || '<br>' || REC.NAMA;

        END IF;

    END LOOP;    

   RETURN RESULT;    

END;

$$;

pg_restore: error: could not execute query: ERROR:  permission denied for schema public
Command was: CREATE FUNCTION public.ambil_bidang_nama_bykode(kodebidang character varying) RETURNS character varying
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$

DECLARE 

tmpTahun VARCHAR(1024);

bidangusaha VARCHAR(1024);

BIDANGUSAHAID VARCHAR(100);

BEGIN

SELECT BIDANG_USAHA_ID INTO BIDANGUSAHAID FROM BIDANG_USAHA WHERE KODE = KODEBIDANG;

FOR i IN 0 .. (LENGTH(BIDANGUSAHAID) / 2) - 1 

    LOOP

    SELECT NAMA into bidangusaha  FROM BIDANG_USAHA WHERE BIDANG_USAHA_ID = SUBSTR(BIDANGUSAHAID, 0, LENGTH(BIDANGUSAHAID) - (i * 2));

        IF i = 0 THEN

            tmpTahun := bidangusaha;

        ELSE 

            tmpTahun := bidangusaha || ' | ' || tmpTahun;

        END IF;

END 

	LOOP;

    RETURN tmpTahun;

END;

$$;

Command was: CREATE FUNCTION public.ambil_bulan(bulanid integer) RETURNS character varying
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$

DECLARE 

tmpTahun VARCHAR(50);

BEGIN

IF(BULANID = 1) THEN

   RETURN 'Januari';    

ELSIF(BULANID = 2) THEN

   RETURN 'Februari';    

ELSIF(BULANID = 3) THEN

   RETURN 'Maret';    

ELSIF(BULANID = 4) THEN

   RETURN 'April';    

ELSIF(BULANID = 5) THEN

   RETURN 'Mei';    

ELSIF(BULANID = 6) THEN

   RETURN 'Juni';    

ELSIF(BULANID = 7) THEN

   RETURN 'Juli';    

ELSIF(BULANID = 8) THEN

   RETURN 'Agustus';    

ELSIF(BULANID = 9) THEN

   RETURN 'September';    

ELSIF(BULANID = 10) THEN

   RETURN 'Oktober';    

ELSIF(BULANID = 11) THEN

   RETURN 'November';    

ELSIF(BULANID = 12) THEN

   RETURN 'Desember';    

ELSE    

   RETURN '';    

END IF;

END;

$$;

Command was: CREATE FUNCTION public.ambil_neraca_keuangan(rekananid integer) RETURNS character varying
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$

DECLARE 

rec RECORD;

RESULT VARCHAR(5000);

BEGIN

    RESULT:='';

    FOR REC IN ( SELECT 'Nomor : ' || MODAL

			    || '<br>Auditor : ' ||  AUDIT_NAMA

			    || '<br>Nomor : ' ||  AUDIT_NOMOR

			    || '<br>Tanggal : ' || TO_CHAR(AUDIT_TANGGAL, 'DD-MM-YYYY') NAMA

					 FROM REKANAN_NERACA A INNER JOIN

					   (SELECT REKANAN_ID, MAX(TAHUN) TAHUN FROM REKANAN_NERACA GROUP BY REKANAN_ID) B

					   ON A.REKANAN_ID = B.REKANAN_ID AND A.TAHUN = B.TAHUN

			    WHERE A.REKANAN_ID = REKANANID)

    LOOP

        IF RESULT IS NULL THEN

            RESULT := REC.NAMA;

        ELSE        

            RESULT := RESULT || '<br>' || REC.NAMA;

        END IF;

    END LOOP;    

   RETURN RESULT;    

END;

$$;

    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$

rec RECORD;

RESULT VARCHAR(5000);

BEGIN

    RESULT:='';

            RESULT := REC.LOKASI;

    END LOOP;

END;

$$;

Command was: CREATE FUNCTION public.ambil_pengalaman_bidang_usaha(rekananpengalamanid integer) RETURNS character varying
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$

DECLARE 

REC RECORD;

tmpTahun VARCHAR(5000);

x NUMERIC := 1; 

bidangusaha VARCHAR(1024);

BEGIN

    tmpTahun:='';

    FOR REC in (SELECT BIDANG_USAHA_ID FROM REKANAN_PENGALAMAN_BIDANG WHERE REKANAN_PENGALAMAN_ID = REKANANPENGALAMANID)

    LOOP

        IF REC.BIDANG_USAHA_ID IS NOT NULL THEN

            SELECT AMBIL_BIDANG_USAHA_NAMA(REC.BIDANG_USAHA_ID) INTO bidangusaha;

            IF x = 1 THEN

                tmpTahun := bidangusaha;

            ELSE 

                tmpTahun := tmpTahun || '<BR/>' || bidangusaha;

            END IF;

            x := x + 1;

         END IF;

    END LOOP;    

   RETURN tmpTahun;    

END;

$$;

    END LOOP;

    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$

Command was: CREATE FUNCTION public.ambil_tenaga_ahli_pendidikan(rekanantenagaahliid integer) RETURNS character varying
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$

DECLARE 

REC RECORD;

tmpTahun VARCHAR(5000);

x NUMERIC := 1; 

bidangusaha VARCHAR(1024);

BEGIN

    tmpTahun:='';

    FOR REC in (SELECT REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PEND_ID FROM REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PEND WHERE REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_ID = REKANANTENAGAAHLIID)

    LOOP

        SELECT  '(' || (SELECT NAMA FROM REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PEND A, PENDIDIKAN B WHERE REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PEND_ID = REC.REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PEND_ID AND A.PENDIDIKAN = CAST(B.PENDIDIKAN_ID AS TEXT)) || '-' || (SELECT JURUSAN FROM REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PEND WHERE REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PEND_ID = REC.REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PEND_ID) || ') ' INTO bidangusaha;

        IF x = 1 THEN

            tmpTahun := bidangusaha;

        ELSE 

            tmpTahun := tmpTahun || '* ' || bidangusaha;

        END IF;

        x := x + 1;

    END LOOP;    

   RETURN tmpTahun;    

END;

$$;

    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$

DECLARE 

REC RECORD;

tmpTahun VARCHAR(5000);

x NUMERIC := 1; 

bidangusaha VARCHAR(1024);

BEGIN

    tmpTahun:='';

    FOR REC in (SELECT REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG_ID FROM REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG WHERE REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_ID = REKANANTENAGAAHLIID)

    LOOP

        SELECT  

        '{%}' || (SELECT PEKERJAAN FROM REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG WHERE REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG_ID = REC.REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG_ID)

    || '-' || (SELECT POSISI FROM REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG WHERE REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG_ID = REC.REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG_ID)

    || '-' || (SELECT PERIODE FROM REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG WHERE REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG_ID = REC.REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG_ID)

        || '-' || (SELECT PENGALAMAN FROM REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG WHERE REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG_ID = REC.REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG_ID)

    || '-' || (SELECT INSTANSI FROM REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG WHERE REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG_ID = REC.REKANAN_TENAGA_AHLI_PENG_ID)

        IF x = 1 THEN

            tmpTahun := bidangusaha;

        END IF;

        x := x + 1;

    END LOOP;    

   RETURN tmpTahun;    

END;

    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$

DECLARE 

REC RECORD;

IDKONVERSI VARCHAR(4000);

x NUMERIC := 1; 

BEGIN

    FOR REC in (SELECT REPLACE(NAMA, ',', ';') NAMA 

				FROM BIDANG_USAHA A 

			WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM (SELECT regexp_split_to_table(NAMAKONVERSI, ',') AS SPLIT_VALUE) X WHERE SPLIT_VALUE = BIDANG_USAHA_ID))

    LOOP        

        IF x = 1 THEN

            IDKONVERSI := REC.NAMA;

        ELSE 

            IDKONVERSI := IDKONVERSI || ',' || REC.NAMA;

        END IF;

        x := x + 1;

    END LOOP;    

   RETURN IDKONVERSI;    

END;

$$;

    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$

DECLARE

--pragma autonomous_transaction;

METODEKUALIFIKASIID INTEGER;

BEGIN

    IF COALESCE(OLD.KIRIM_PENAWARAN, '0') = '1' THEN

        RETURN NEW;

    END IF;

    IF COALESCE(NEW.KIRIM_PENAWARAN, '0') = '1' THEN

        NEW.KIRIM_PENAWARAN_TANGGAL := NOW();

    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;

--EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN

    --raise_application_error(-20001,'An error was encountered - '||SQLCODE||' -ERROR- '||SQLERRM);

END;

$$;

pg_restore: error: could not execute query: ERROR:  permission denied for schema public
Command was: CREATE FUNCTION public.update_negosiasi() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$

DECLARE

--pragma autonomous_transaction;

NILAITOTAL NUMERIC;

BEGIN

    IF NOT NEW.SETUJUI = '1' THEN

        RETURN NEW;

    END IF;

    SELECT SUM((UNIT_PRICE * QUANTITY)) INTO NILAITOTAL FROM PAKET_NEGOSIASI WHERE PAKET_ID = NEW.PAKET_ID;

    NILAITOTAL := COALESCE(NILAITOTAL, 0);

    UPDATE PAKET SET NILAI_NEGOSIASI = NILAITOTAL WHERE PAKET_ID = NEW.PAKET_ID;

    NEW.SETUJUI_DATE := CURRENT_DATE;

    RETURN NEW;

    --COMMIT;

--EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN

    --raise_application_error(-20001,'An error was encountered - '||SQLCODE||' -ERROR- '||SQLERRM);

END;

$$;

pg_restore: error: could not execute query: ERROR:  permission denied for schema public
Command was: CREATE FUNCTION public.update_pascakualifikasi() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$

DECLARE

--pragma autonomous_transaction;

METODEKUALIFIKASIID INTEGER;

BEGIN

    SELECT PAKET_METODE_KUALIFIKASI_ID INTO METODEKUALIFIKASIID FROM PAKET WHERE PAKET_ID = NEW.PAKET_ID;

    IF METODEKUALIFIKASIID = 1 THEN

        RETURN NEW;

    END IF;

    IF NEW.LULUS_PENDAFTARAN = 1 THEN

        NEW.LULUS_ADMINISTRASI := 1;

        NEW.LULUS_KUALIFIKASI := 1;

    ELSE

        NEW.LULUS_ADMINISTRASI := 0;

        NEW.LULUS_KUALIFIKASI := 0;     

    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;

--EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN

    --raise_application_error(-20001,'An error was encountered - '||SQLCODE||' -ERROR- '||SQLERRM);

END;

$$;

    a.jam_awal,

Command was: CREATE VIEW public.paket_negosiasi_lot AS
 SELECT a.paket_id,
        CASE
            WHEN ((a.item_parent)::text = '0'::text) THEN a.item_child
            ELSE a.item_parent
        END AS item_parent,
    sum(((a.quantity)::numeric * b.unit_price)) AS jumlah_awal,
    sum(((a.quantity)::numeric * c.unit_price)) AS jumlah_akhir,
    sum(b.biaya_kirim) AS biaya_kirim_awal,
    sum(c.biaya_kirim) AS biaya_kirim
   FROM ((public.paket_penawaran a
     JOIN public.rekanan_paket_penawaran b ON (((a.paket_penawaran_id = b.paket_penawaran_id) AND (( SELECT x.rekanan_id
           FROM public.paket_rekanan x
          WHERE (x.paket_rekanan_id = b.paket_rekanan_id)) = a.rekanan_id_pemenang))))
     JOIN public.paket_negosiasi c ON ((a.paket_penawaran_id = c.paket_penawaran_id)))
  GROUP BY a.paket_id,
        CASE
            WHEN ((a.item_parent)::text = '0'::text) THEN a.item_child
            ELSE a.item_parent
        END
  ORDER BY a.paket_id;

   FROM public.rekanan_pengurus a
  WHERE (((upper((a.jabatan)::text) ~~ '%DIREKTUR UTAMA%'::text) OR (upper((a.jabatan)::text) ~~ '%DIREKTUR  UTAMA%'::text) OR (upper((a.jabatan)::text) ~~ 'PRESIDEN%'::text) OR (upper((a.jabatan)::text) ~~ 'DIRUT%'::text)) AND (NOT (upper((a.jabatan)::text) ~~ '%KOMISARIS%'::text)))

    a.rekanan_id,
    a.nama,
    a.jabatan
   FROM public.rekanan_pengurus a
  WHERE (upper((a.jabatan)::text) ~~ 'DIREKTUR'::text)
UNION ALL

    a.rekanan_id,

    skor,
    jumlah_nilai,
    rata2_skor,
    (presentasi_persen * rata2_skor) AS total_skor,
    (skor / (20)::numeric) AS skor_ui,
    ((skor / (20)::numeric) * presentasi_persen) AS skor_tertimbang_ui
   FROM ( SELECT v.paket_id,
            v.rekanan_id,
            v.ppt_parent_id,
            v.contractingrekananid,
            v.presentasi,
            (v.presentasi / (100)::numeric) AS presentasi_persen,
            v.skor,
            v.jumlah_nilai,
            (v.skor / (v.jumlah_nilai)::numeric) AS rata2_skor
           FROM ( SELECT a.paket_id,
                    a.rekanan_id,
                    a.ppt_parent_id,
                    a.contractingrekananid,
                    a.presentasi,
                    sum(a.nilai) AS skor,
                    count(a.nilai) AS jumlah_nilai
                   FROM public.paket_penilaian_rekanan a
                  GROUP BY a.paket_id, a.rekanan_id, a.ppt_parent_id, a.contractingrekananid, a.presentasi) v) q;

    tgl_approve_permohonan_kepala_pengadaan,
    ((tgl_approve_permohonan)::timestamp without time zone - (tgl_teruskan_permohonan)::timestamp without time zone) AS lama_usulan_approval,
    tgl_proses_paket,
    tgl_publish_penetapan_pemenang,
    tgl_penunjukan_pengelola_kontrak,
    ((tgl_publish_penetapan_pemenang)::timestamp without time zone - (tgl_approve_permohonan_kepala_pengadaan)::timestamp without time zone) AS lama_pelaksanaan_pengadaan,
    tgl_selesai_kontrak,
    ((tgl_selesai_kontrak)::timestamp without time zone - (tgl_penunjukan_pengelola_kontrak)::timestamp without time zone) AS lama_pengelola_kontrak
   FROM ( SELECT a_1.permohonan_paket_id,
            c.paket_id,
            a_1.tahun_anggaran,
            a_1.nama,
            a_1.user_login_id,
            a_1.perkiraan_biaya_harga,
            date_part('year'::text, a_1.rencana_pengadaan) AS tahun_rencana_pengadaan,
            date_part('month'::text, a_1.rencana_pengadaan) AS bulan_rencana_pengadaan,
            ( SELECT to_char(x.created_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'::text) AS to_char
                   FROM public.rekam_jejak x
                  WHERE ((a_1.permohonan_paket_id = x.permohonan_paket_id) AND ((x.flow)::text = '101'::text))
                  ORDER BY x.rekamid
                 LIMIT 1) AS tgl_input_permohonan,
            ( SELECT to_char(x.created_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'::text) AS to_char
                   FROM public.rekam_jejak x
                  WHERE ((a_1.permohonan_paket_id = x.permohonan_paket_id) AND ((x.flow)::text = '2'::text))
                  ORDER BY x.rekamid
                 LIMIT 1) AS tgl_teruskan_permohonan,
            ( SELECT to_char(x.created_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'::text) AS to_char
                   FROM public.rekam_jejak x
                  WHERE ((a_1.permohonan_paket_id = x.permohonan_paket_id) AND ((x.flow)::text = '3'::text))
                  ORDER BY x.rekamid
                 LIMIT 1) AS tgl_approve_permohonan,
                CASE
                    WHEN (( SELECT to_char(x.created_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'::text) AS to_char
                       FROM public.rekam_jejak x
                      WHERE ((a_1.permohonan_paket_id = x.permohonan_paket_id) AND ((x.flow)::text = '5'::text))
                      ORDER BY x.rekamid
                     LIMIT 1) IS NOT NULL) THEN ( SELECT to_char(x.created_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'::text) AS to_char
                       FROM public.rekam_jejak x
                      WHERE ((a_1.permohonan_paket_id = x.permohonan_paket_id) AND ((x.flow)::text = '5'::text))
                      ORDER BY x.rekamid
                     LIMIT 1)
                    ELSE to_char((a_1.pic_date)::timestamp with time zone, 'YYYY-MM-DD'::text)
                END AS tgl_teruskan_permohonan_kepala_pengadaan,
            ( SELECT to_char(x.created_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'::text) AS to_char
                   FROM public.rekam_jejak x
                  WHERE ((a_1.permohonan_paket_id = x.permohonan_paket_id) AND ((x.flow)::text = '6'::text))
                  ORDER BY x.rekamid
                 LIMIT 1) AS tgl_approve_permohonan_kepala_pengadaan,
            ( SELECT to_char(x.created_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'::text) AS to_char
                   FROM public.rekam_jejak x
                  WHERE ((c.paket_id = x.paket_id) AND ((x.flow)::text = '8'::text))
                  ORDER BY x.rekamid
                 LIMIT 1) AS tgl_proses_paket,
            ( SELECT to_char(x.created_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'::text) AS to_char
                   FROM public.rekam_jejak x
                  WHERE ((c.paket_id = x.paket_id) AND ((x.flow)::text = '24'::text))
                  ORDER BY x.rekamid
                 LIMIT 1) AS tgl_publish_penetapan_pemenang,
            ( SELECT to_char(x.created_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'::text) AS to_char
                   FROM public.rekam_jejak x
                  WHERE ((c.paket_id = x.paket_id) AND ((x.flow)::text = '30'::text))
                  ORDER BY x.rekamid
                 LIMIT 1) AS tgl_penunjukan_pengelola_kontrak,
            ( SELECT to_char(x.created_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'::text) AS to_char
                   FROM public.rekam_jejak x
                  WHERE ((c.paket_id = x.paket_id) AND ((x.flow)::text = '267'::text))
                  ORDER BY x.rekamid
                 LIMIT 1) AS tgl_selesai_kontrak
           FROM ((public.permohonan_paket a_1
             JOIN public.permohonan_paket_analisa b ON ((a_1.permohonan_paket_analisa_id = b.permohonan_paket_analisa_id)))
             LEFT JOIN public.paket c ON ((a_1.permohonan_paket_id = c.permohonan_paket_id)))
          WHERE (c.paket_metode_lelang_id = ANY (ARRAY[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 8, 10, 11]))
          ORDER BY a_1.permohonan_paket_id DESC) a;

    tgl_permohonan_approve,
